The diagonal element depends on those off-diagonal\n// results, so it is computed once all of them are finished.\n\nbool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    // A is stored in row-major order\n    bool notPositiveDefinite = false;\n\n    #pragma omp parallel default(shared)\n    {\n        for (size_t i = 0; The diagonal element depends on those off-diagonal\n// results, so it is computed once all of them are finished.\n\nbool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    // A is stored in row-major order\n    bool notPositiveDefinite = false;\n\n    #pragma omp parallel default(shared)\n    {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n            if (notPositiveDefinite) {\n                break;\n            }\n\n            // Off-diagonal elements of row i: independent across j, so parallelize.\n            #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 32)\n            for (size_t j = 0; j < i; ++j) {\n                double sum = 0.0;\n                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {\n                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];\n                }\n                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];\n            }\n\n            // Diagonal element depends on the off-diagonal values just computed.\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            #pragma omp for reduction(+:sum) schedule(static)\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < i; ++k) {\n                sum += A[i * n + k] * A[i * n + k];\n            }\n\n            #pragma omp single\n            {\n                const double val = A[i * n + i] - sum;\n                if (val <= 0.0) {\n                    // Matrix is not positive definite\n                    printf(\"Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\\n\", i);\n                    notPositiveDefinite = true;\n                } else {\n                    A[i * n + i] = sqrt(val);\n                }\n            }\n\n            if (notPositiveDefinite) {\n                break;\n            }\n\n            // Zero out upper triangular part of row i.\n            #pragma omp for schedule(static)\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; i < n * n; ++i) {\n        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;\n    }\n    \n    // Compute A = B * B^T\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) collapse(2)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i * n + i] += n;\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {\n    // Validate by computing L * L^T and comparing with original matrix\n    \n    std::vector<double> reconstructed(n * n);\n    \n    // Compute L * L^T\n    for (size_t i = 0; For each panel:\n//   1. The small diagonal block is factorized sequentially (unblocked).\n//   2. The panel below the diagonal block is solved via forward substitution;\n//      each row of the panel is independent of the others, so this is\n//      parallelized across rows.\n//   3. The trailing submatrix is updated by subtracting the outer product of\n//      the panel with itself; each (row, col) entry is independent, so this\n//      is parallelized across rows as well.\n// This exposes coarse-grained, independent parallel work at every step,\n// unlike the strictly sequential column-by-column dependency chain of the\n// unblocked left-looking algorithm.\n\nstatic constexpr size_t BLOCK_SIZE = 128;\n\nbool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    // A is stored in row-major order; only the lower triangle is read/written\n    // until the final zeroing pass.\n    bool notPositiveDefinite = false;\n\n    for (size_t kb = 0; kb < n && !notPositiveDefinite; kb += BLOCK_SIZE) {\n        const size_t blockEnd = std::min(kb + BLOCK_SIZE, n);\n\n        // Factorize the diagonal block in place (small, sequential unblocked Cholesky).\n        for (size_t i = kb; i < blockEnd && !notPositiveDefinite; ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = kb; j <= i; ++j) {\n                double sum = 0.0;\n                for (size_t k = kb; k < j; ++k) {\n                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];\n                }\n                if (i == j) {\n                    const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;\n                    if (val <= 0.0) {\n                        // Matrix is not positive definite\n                        printf(\"Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\\n\", j);\n                        notPositiveDefinite = true;\n                        break;\n                    }\n                    A[j * n + j] = sqrt(val);\n                } else {\n                    A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        if (notPositiveDefinite) {\n            break;\n        }\n\n        // Panel solve: rows below the diagonal block are independent of each other.\n        #pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic, 8)\n        for (size_t i = blockEnd; i < n; ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = kb; j < blockEnd; ++j) {\n                double sum = 0.0;\n                for (size_t k = kb; k < j; ++k) {\n                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];\n                }\n                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Trailing submatrix update: A[i,j] -= L_panel[i,:] . L_panel[j,:], independent per (i,j).\n        #pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic, 8)\n        for (size_t i = blockEnd; i < n; ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = blockEnd; j <= i; ++j) {\n                double sum = 0.0;\n                for (size_t k = kb; k < blockEnd; ++k) {\n                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];\n                }\n                A[i * n + j] -= sum;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Zero out the upper triangular part.\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return !notPositiveDefinite;\n}"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":6792,"cache_read_input_tokens":42289,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":6792},"output_tokens":20,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ec21a405-e2a0-49d7-bfcc-a69c88812e8e","uuid":"d8404459-856b-4423-a513-b839b4ad24a4","timestamp":"2026-09-01T23:19:35.587Z","request_id":"req_011CedaKYAcmT3zPGwsgmdpg"}

The diagonal element depends on those off-diagonal\n// results, so it is computed once all of them are finished.\n\nbool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    // A is stored in row-major order\n    bool notPositiveDefinite = false;\n    double diagSum = 0.0;\n\n    #pragma omp parallel default(shared)\n    {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {\n        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;\n    }\n    \n    // Compute A = B * B^T\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) collapse(2)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i * n + i] += n;\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {\n    // Validate by computing L * L^T and comparing with original matrix\n    \n    std::vector<double> reconstructed(n * n);\n    \n    // Compute L * L^T\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) collapse(2)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {\n        const double error = fabs(reconstructed[i] - A_orig[i]);\n        maxError = std::max(maxError, error);\n\n        const double rel = error / (fabs(A_orig[i]) + 1e-10);\n        relError = std::max(relError, rel);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Max absolute error: %.10e\\n\", maxError);\n    printf(\"Max relative error: %.10e\\n\", relError);\n    \n    // Check if error is within tolerance\n    if (relError > 1e-6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: relative error too large\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t n = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; The diagonal element depends on those off-diagonal","-// results, so it is computed once all of them are finished.","+// The matrix is processed in panels of BLOCK_SIZE columns. For each panel:","+//   1. The small diagonal block is factorized sequentially (unblocked).","+//   2. The panel below the diagonal block is solved via forward substitution;","+//      each row of the panel is independent of the others, so this is","+//      parallelized across rows.","+//   3.
